During development, we identified two fundamental challenges that severely impair delayed/false trigger annotation accuracy: (1) Extreme class imbalance where delayed/false triggers are overwhelmed by true triggers; (2) Asymmetric label noise where mislabeled majority samples (true triggers) suppress minority samples (delayed/false triggers) learning. To overcome these challenges, we propose two key innovations: (1) Specific data augmentation that synthesizes realistic samples by manipulating focal target attributes, transplanting ego-vehicle dynamics, and masking non-focal agents; (2) noise suppression using stable hardness estimation and probe-guided adaptive threshold to clean mislabeled true trigger samples. Crucially, we deploy our model as a practical annotation system with full-stack architecture, efficiently identifying critical delayed/false triggers from thousands of daily AEB events. Production results demonstrate 80% improvement in recall of delayed/false triggers and 50% reduction in manual workload. Beyond immediate gains, the system enables continuous self-improvement through accumulated high-quality annotations, establishing a necessary data foundation for on-vehicle AEB system optimization

</details>


### [182] [FAST-LIVGO: A Degeneracy-Robust LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-GNSS Fusion Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19190)
*Zhiyu Chen,Chunran Zheng,Jiayu Wen,XiaoLei Zhang,Jiaming Xu,Feng Pan,Yukang Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波的紧耦合LiDAR-惯性-视觉-GNSS融合框架，通过动态时间规整的时空对齐模块、多普勒频移和固定锚点时间差分载波相位观测模型，以及退化感知的双模式异常值剔除策略，显著减少了长距离大规模动态环境中的累积漂移和地图重影，在公共M3DGR数据集和自建20m/s固定翼无人机数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIVO系统在长期大规模高动态环境中存在累积漂移，且在几何退化或无纹理场景中可能失效；GNSS辅助融合框架依赖LiDAR或视觉里程计进行状态预测和异常值剔除，在里程计退化时易受影响。

Method: 1) 基于误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波的紧耦合LiDAR-惯性-视觉-GNSS融合框架；2) 使用动态时间规整的在线时空对齐模块处理高动态条件；3) 基于多普勒频移和固定锚点时间差分载波相位的观测模型，提供毫米级相对约束；4) 退化感知的双模式异常值剔除策略，根据LIVO退化程度在LIVO先验引导剔除和GNSS辅助恢复之间切换。

Result: 在公共M3DGR数据集和自建20m/s固定翼无人机数据集上，系统减少了累积漂移和地图重影，在精度和鲁棒性方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧耦合融合框架有效解决了长期大规模高动态环境中的漂移和退化问题，提升了状态估计和建图的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robust state estimation and mapping in long-term, large-scale, and highly dynamic environments remains a key challenge in robotics. Existing LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) systems achieve strong local accuracy but suffer from accumulated drift over long distances and may fail in geometrically degraded or textureless scenes. Meanwhile, GNSS-aided fusion frameworks often rely on LiDAR or visual odometry for state prediction and outlier rejection, making them vulnerable when odometry degenerates. To address these limitations, we propose a tightly coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-GNSS fusion framework based on an Error-State Iterated Kalman Filter. An online spatiotemporal alignment module using Dynamic Time Warping is introduced for highly dynamic conditions. To better exploit GNSS precision, we develop observation models based on Doppler shifts and fixed-anchor Time-Differenced Carrier Phase, providing millimeter-level relative constraints without augmenting historical anchor states. We further design a degeneracy-aware dual-mode outlier rejection strategy that switches between LIVO-prior-guided rejection and GNSS-aided recovery according to the LIVO degeneracy level. Experiments on the public M3DGR dataset and a custom 20~m/s fixed-wing UAV dataset demonstrate that our system reduces accumulated drift and map ghosting, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [183] [Invertible Neural Network Adapter for One-Step Flow Matching in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19194)
*Yu Zhang,Kangyi Ji,Yongxiang Zou,Rongtao Xu,Feng Zheng,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可逆神经网络和流匹配公式的机器人操作适配器，通过单步去噪过程生成高维动作，在保持任务性能的同时将推理延迟从110ms降至61ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的迭代流匹配策略虽然能生成高质量动作，但推理复杂度高、计算开销大，难以满足机器人实时操作的需求。本文旨在设计一种能够通过单步推理即可生成精确高维动作的轻量化框架，提升推理效率同时不牺牲动作质量。

Method: 基于流匹配公式构建可逆神经网络适配器，将动作生成轨迹约束在可逆潜空间中，通过单步去噪过程完成动作合成。该框架以多模态观测（视觉、语言、本体感知）为条件，实现从观测到高维动作的高效映射。

Result: 在多个仿真基准测试中达到或接近最先进水平；在真实机器人平台上，VLA模型推理延迟从110ms降至61ms（降低约44.5%），同时保持稳定的任务性能。

Conclusion: 可逆神经网络结合流匹配公式能够有效降低推理复杂度，实现高效且高质量的单步动作生成，为机器人操作中的实时决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an invertible neural network adapter for general robotic manipulation, designed to generate precise high-dimensional actions conditioned on multimodal observations, including visual, linguistic, and proprioceptive inputs, through a one-step denoising process. Built upon a flow-matching formulation, the proposed adapter effectively constrains the action generation trajectory within an invertible latent space, thereby enabling efficient and high-quality dexterous action synthesis with only a single inference step. Compared with conventional iterative flow-matching policies, the proposed framework substantially reduces inference complexity while maintaining strong action prediction accuracy and stability. Extensive experiments are conducted across a diverse set of simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic platforms to evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Across simulation benchmarks, the proposed adapter consistently demonstrates superior or near state-of-the-art performance on a wide range of manipulation tasks. Furthermore, real-world experiments reveal a significant improvement in inference efficiency for vision-language-action (VLA) models, reducing the average inference latency from 110 ms to 61 ms while maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [184] [Constant Time-Delay Leader Following with Neural Networks and Invariant Extended Kalman Filters for Arbitrary Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19227)
*Luka Antonyshyn,Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需车间通信、公共坐标系或全局定位的车辆队列恒定时延轨迹跟踪方法，融合概率序列到序列神经网络与不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行轨迹预测预热，并结合几何模型预测控制器在SE(2)流形上实现任意非线性轨迹的高精度跟踪，在仿真和实车实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决车辆队列在缺乏车间通信、公共坐标系和全局定位条件下的恒定时延轨迹跟踪问题，减少对专家领域知识的依赖。

Method: 将概率Seq2Seq神经网络与IEKF集成用于轨迹预测预热，结合几何模型预测控制器在SE(2)流形上利用流形基轨迹预测，实现任意非线性轨迹跟踪。

Result: 通过纯IEKF基线、基于学习的方法和真实轨迹在运动学仿真和实车实验中对比，验证了所提方法在长时延下仍具有优越性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理任意非线性轨迹和变化速度/运动模式，在无通信/全局定位条件下实现高精度轨迹跟踪，且降低了对专家知识的依赖。

Abstract: This paper proposes a constant time-delay trajectory tracking method for vehicle convoys operating without inter-vehicle communication, a common coordinate system, or global positioning. The method integrates a probabilistic sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) neural network with an invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) to warm-start the prediction process, allowing accurate estimation of a leader vehicle's relative trajectory on the SE(2) manifold. A geometric model predictive controller is further incorporated to fully exploit the manifold-based trajectory predictions for improved control performance. The system can handle arbitrary nonlinear trajectories with varying speeds and motion profiles while reducing the need for expert-based domain knowledge for the design of trajectory following systems, even under long trajectory delays. The effectiveness of the method is validated through comparisons with a pure IEKF baseline, learning-based methods, and the ground-truth trajectory in kinematic simulations, as well as in experiments using real robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [185] [Mobile Pedipulation for Object Sliding via Hierarchical Control on a Wheeled Bipedal Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19233)
*Yue Qin,Yulun Zhuang,Zelin Shen,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次化控制框架，使轮式双足机器人能够利用其轮式腿执行平面物体滑动任务（pedipulation）。该方法基于三刚体（TRB）降阶动力学模型构建非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC），显式考虑了髋关节侧摆自由度和多种轮-地接触模式。NMPC同时调控机器人 locomotion 与交互力，并通过轨迹优化的运动规划器生成含粘滑过渡的参考运动。硬件实验验证了两种代表性pedipulation动作：机器人通过 scoot 动作从桌下取出1kg物体，并通过滑动动作移动4kg物体0.228m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使轮式双足机器人能够利用其轮式腿执行平面物体推拉/滑动任务（pedipulation），拓展机器人的操作能力，使其在无需额外机械臂的情况下完成物体搬运等实用任务。

Method: 提出层次化控制框架：1) 基于三刚体（TRB）降阶动力学模型构建NMPC，显式建模髋关节侧摆自由度及多种轮-地接触模式；2) NMPC同时优化机器人 locomotion 与物体交互力；3) 采用轨迹优化的运动规划器生成含粘滑过渡（stick-slip）的参考运动。

Result: 硬件实验验证了两种pedipulation动作：scooting（从桌下取出1kg物体）和 lateral sliding（滑动4kg物体0.228m距离）。

Conclusion: 所提层次化控制框架使轮式双足机器人能够有效执行物体滑动操作，实现了 locomotion 与操作任务的耦合控制。

Abstract: In this letter, we present a hierarchical control framework that enables wheeled bipedal robots to perform planar object sliding tasks with their wheeled legs. The proposed approach formulates a nonlinear model predictive controller (NMPC) based on a reduced-order three rigid bodies (TRB) dynamical model that explicitly accounts for the hip roll degree of freedom and multiple wheel-environment contact modes, which is essential for lateral stepping and pedipulation tasks. Within this framework, the NMPC simultaneously regulates robot locomotion and interaction forces, allowing the robot to stably execute both rolling and object manipulation behaviors. A trajectory-optimization-based robot-object motion planner is developed to generate reference motions that incorporate stick-slip transitions in ground-object contact. Two representative pedipulation motions, namely scooting and lateral sliding, are validated through real-world hardware experiments, in which the robot successfully retrieves a 1 kg object from under a desk and slides a 4 kg object over a distance of 0.228 m via scooting.

</details>


### [186] [Shape Sensing of Continuum Robots using Direct Laser Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19265)
*Amber K. Rothe,Nidhi Malhotra,Jaydev P. Desai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用直接激光写入技术在同一激光加工平台上将柔性连续体关节和应变传感器一体化制造，传感器通过线性和非线性模型预测关节角度，误差低至1.76度，并实现了闭环控制下追踪误差小于3度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人因柔性导致形状估计困难，现有传感方法（成像、光学、磁感、电阻感）存在局限，需要一种更集成的传感方案。

Method: 利用直接激光写入（DLW）技术在同一激光器上同时加工柔性连续体关节和石墨烯应变传感器，实现一体化制造；采用线性和非线性模型对传感器进行标定和角度预测。

Result: 传感器预测关节角度误差低至1.76度；闭环控制下追踪误差小于3度。

Conclusion: DLW传感器可有效用于连续体机器人的形状估计与闭环控制，为微创手术提供了一种集成化、低成本的传感方案。

Abstract: Continuum robots offer a promising approach for minimally invasive and natural-orifice surgical procedures due to their inherent compliance and dexterity. However, this flexibility also makes estimating the current shape of the robot challenging. Several approaches have been used to reconstruct the shape of these robots, including imaging, optical sensing, magnetic sensing, and resistive sensing. Strain sensors fabricated using direct laser writing (DLW) could provide an alternative sensing method. This technique involves using a laser to induce carbonization of certain polymers to create graphene patterns, such as strain sensors. In this paper, we demonstrate how a flexible continuum joint and a DLW sensor can be machined as one monolithic structure using the same laser and the same setup. The fabricated sensors are characterized using linear and nonlinear models, which are used to predict the joint angle with error as low as 1.76 degrees. Furthermore, we demonstrate how a DLW sensor can be used to implement closed-loop control in a robotic joint, achieving tracking error under 3 degrees.

</details>


### [187] [A Mixed-Reality Testbed for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19267)
*H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Ehsan Sabouni,Emrullah Celik,Zean Wan,Damola Ajeyemi,Christos G. Cassandras,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合现实硬件在环自主车辆测试平台，将物理移动机器人测试平台与高保真仿真环境无缝集成，支持感知、规划与控制算法的安全关键场景验证，并引入基于控制屏障函数的在线学习控制器，弥合仿真与真实硬件部署之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主车辆测试方法在仿真与真实硬件部署之间存在鸿沟，缺乏既能提供高保真虚拟环境又能集成物理硬件和无线通信的混合现实测试平台，难以有效验证安全关键场景下的多智能体系统。

Method: 采用混合现实硬件在环架构，将物理移动机器人测试床与高保真仿真环境结合；利用物理机器人搭载多模态传感器融入真实感虚拟环境；集成无线通信实现车联网连接；通过物理机器人与虚拟智能体组合支持大规模多智能体系统；提出结合感知、规划与基于控制屏障函数的新型在线学习控制器框架。

Result: 实验验证了所提测试平台的关键功能，展示了其在安全关键场景下对CAVs算法验证的有效性，以及在线学习CBF控制器在保障安全方面的性能。

Conclusion: 该混合现实硬件在环测试平台能够有效弥合仿真与真实世界硬件部署之间的差距，为自主车辆和车联网多智能体系统的安全验证提供了一种可靠的实验手段。

Abstract: We propose a mixed-reality, hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) testbed for autonomous vehicles that seamlessly integrates a physical testbed of mobile robots with a high-fidelity simulation environment. The virtual simulation enables the creation of diverse, safety-critical driving scenarios to validate state-of-the-art perception, planning, and control algorithms, while augmenting simulations with physical robots equipped with multimodal sensors in photorealistic virtual environments further facilitating rigorous validation. Our testbed also features vehicular connectivity using wireless communication and can accommodate a large number of agents through the combination of physical robots and virtual simulated agents, supporting research on multi-agent systems including Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs). Finally, we present a safety-guaranteed framework combining perception, planning and a novel online learning-based controller using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) for CAVs. Experiments using the proposed framework are used to validate and demonstrate the key functionalities and the overall utility of the testbed to bridge the gap between simulation and real-world hardware deployment.

</details>


### [188] [Observability and Consistency Analysis for Visual-Inertial Navigation with Anchored Feature Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19307)
*Mitchell Cohen,Vassili Korotkine,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于滤波的视觉-惯性导航系统（VINS）中采用锚定特征表示的可观测性和一致性特性。锚定地标参数化使VINS的不可观测子空间独立于估计的地标状态，从而无需额外修改即可提高估计器的一致性。但不可观测子空间仍依赖于导航状态，需额外的一致性增强技术。实验表明锚定特征表示能显著提升一致性，尤其在特征初始化较差时表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于滤波的VINS中锚定特征表示对可观测性和一致性的影响，探索其对估计器一致性的改善作用及局限性。

Method: 理论分析锚定特征表示下VINS的可观测性子空间特性，提出两种增强一致性的方法，并通过仿真和TUM-VI数据集实验验证。

Result: 锚定特征表示的不可观测子空间独立于地标状态，但依赖于导航状态；锚定特征表示可提升一致性，尤其在特征初始化差时效果显著；真实实验中锚定特征表示性能可与采用全局特征表示的一致性增强估计器相媲美。

Conclusion: 锚定特征参数化对VINS估计器的一致性有显著改善效果，是一种实用的VINS特征表示方法。

Abstract: This paper presents an analysis of the observability and consistency properties of filtering-based visual-inertial navigation systems (VINS) that utilize anchored feature representations. The unobservable subspace of VINS with anchored landmark parameterizations is shown to be independent of the estimated landmark state, which leads to improved estimator consistency properties without any additional modifications. However, the unobservable subspace is still found to depend on the estimated navigation state, necessitating additional consistency-enforcing techniques. Two methods to improve the consistency of VINS with anchored feature representations are presented. Simulation results showcase that all estimators employing anchored feature paramterizations exhibit improved consistency properties compared to algorithms that estimate features resolved in a global reference frame, especially in scenarios where feature initialization may be poor. Real-world experiments on the TUM-VI dataset showcase that the use of anchored feature representations alone can yield comparable performance to consistency-improved estimators employing a global feature representation, demonstrating the benefit of using anchored feature parameterizations for VINS.

</details>


### [189] [Modeling Branches for Active Manipulation using Iterative Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19314)
*Madhav Rijal,Rashik Shrestha,Trevor Smith,Yu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过迭代估计材料参数来建模多样化植物枝条的方法，结合有限元仿真和变形感知运动规划，实现精细的枝条操纵，在30次试验中平均减少35.69%的变形能，路径长度仅增加8.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人需要在密集 foliage 中对植物进行重新定位、稳定和清除视觉障碍，但枝条具有多样化的几何和材料特性，现有方法难以实现精细的枝条操纵。

Method: 从点云数据构建四面体枝条模型，利用有限元法模拟其行为；通过真实观测的变形数据迭代估计枝条材料参数；然后使用变形感知运动规划器计算最优路径，以移动和稳定枝条至另一机器人视野内。

Result: 在30次跨不同几何和材料特性的枝条试验中，该方法平均降低变形能35.69%，路径长度仅增加8.10%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效支持农业机器人对多样化枝条进行精细操纵，在显著减少变形的前提下实现合理的路径规划。

Abstract: This study presents a method for modeling diverse plant branches by iteratively estimating material parameters to support delicate branch manipulation. Branch manipulation is necessary in agricultural robotics for plant repositioning, stabilizing, and clearing visual obstructions in dense foliage. The proposed method builds a tetrahedral branch model from point-cloud data and simulates its behavior using the finite element method. Using real observed deformation data, it iteratively estimates branch parameters and then computes an optimal path with a deformation-aware motion planner to move and stabilize branches within another robot's field of view. Across 30 trials on branches with varying geometries and material properties, the proposed method reduced the deformation energy by 35.69% while increasing the path length by 8.10% on average.

</details>


### [190] [Do as I Do: Dexterous Manipulation Data from Everyday Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19333)
*Bhawna Paliwal,Haritheja Etukuru,William Liang,Pieter Abbeel,Nur Muhammad Mahi Shafiullah,Jitendra Malik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DO AS I DO算法，从单目RGB人类视频中重建人手-物体交互并重定向到多指灵巧机器人手，实现从人类视频中可扩展生成机器人操作数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作数据生成方法在类人平台（如多指灵巧手）上扩展性差，而人类视频虽丰富但存在手-物交互估计困难和人类到机器人具身鸿沟问题，亟需一种能从无约束单目RGB视频中可扩展生成机器人操作数据的方法。

Method: DO AS I DO算法包含两阶段：首先从多种视角（自我中心和外中心）的野生视频中重建手-物交互；然后将这些交互估计重定向为真实世界可执行的动作序列，生成机器人完整操作数据。

Result: 在包含真实标注的数据集和在线收集的视频片段数据集上，DO AS I DO在手-物交互估计和从RGB视频提取灵巧操作轨迹方面均优于先前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了从单目RGB人类视频可扩展生成灵巧机器人操作数据的可行性，并为从业者收集人类操作数据提出了有效性指南。

Abstract: How can we scalably generate data for robotic manipulation, especially on human-like platforms such as dexterous multi-fingered hands? Learning from human videos has recently emerged as a likely answer to this question. However, difficulties in estimating hand-object interaction and crossing the human-to-robot embodiment gap have hindered the adoption of abundant monocular RGB-only human videos as the primary source of robot manipulation data. In this work, we present DO AS I DO, an algorithm to reconstruct and retarget monocular RGB human videos to multi-fingered dexterous robotic hands. DO AS I DO reconstructs hand-object interactions from various egocentric and exocentric in-the-wild video sources. The algorithm then retargets these hand-object interaction estimates into a sequence of actions executable in the real world, yielding robot-complete manipulation data from disparate human videos. Overall, DO AS I DO outperforms previous state of the art in estimating hand-object interactions and extracting dexterous manipulation trajectories from RGB videos, as we show in experiments on datasets with ground truths and on a dataset of video clips collected online. Our experiments enable us to propose an efficacy playbook for practitioners collecting human data for manipulation.

</details>


### [191] [Zero-Shot Long-Horizon Dexterous Manipulation via Multi-View 3D-Grounded VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19340)
*Jisoo Kim,Sangwon Baik,Taeksoo Kim,Sungjoo Kim,Junyoung Lee,Mingi Choi,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种零样本框架，通过多视角RGB图像、视觉语言模型（VLM）和3D关键点提升，实现长时程灵巧操作的指令到3D任务规划的转换，无需端到端训练，在真实实验中优于单视角RGB-D和微调VLA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端策略和单视角RGB-D方法在长时程灵巧操作中3D定位精度和执行可靠性不足，且缺乏对未见物体和新场景的零样本泛化能力。

Method: 1) 使用VLM生成参考帧任务基础和2D关键点；2) 通过多视角融合（三角测量+参考视角射线投票）将2D关键点提升为3D关键点；3) 针对工具使用，检索原子动作并对齐6D工具轨迹；4) 针对灵巧执行，将提升的抓取关键点扩展为任务条件抓取区域，并用臂手运动生成器生成抓取-运动对；5) 通过闭环状态验证和重新规划实现长时程操作。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，该方法在3D定位精度和执行可靠性上优于单视角RGB-D定位和微调VLA基线，并在未见物体和新场景中实现了零样本长时程操作。

Conclusion: 通过多视角VLM定位融合和模块化任务规划，无需端到端训练即可实现长时程灵巧操作的零样本泛化，验证了跨视角几何一致性对3D定位的重要性。

Abstract: We present a zero-shot framework for long-horizon dexterous manipulation that grounds language instructions into executable 3D task plans from calibrated multi-view RGB images. Rather than training an end-to-end policy, our system uses a vision-language model (VLM) to produce reference-frame task grounding and primitive-level 2D keypoints, then lifts them into 3D via multi-view fusion. This lifting combines triangulation of view-wise VLM groundings with reference-view ray voting, which searches along a semantic camera ray for geometrically consistent candidates across neighboring views. The resulting 3D keypoints support both pick-and-place and tool-use: for tool-use, we retrieve an object-centric atomic action corresponding to the inferred skill category and align its stored 6D tool trajectory to the scene; for dexterous execution, we expand the lifted grasp keypoint into a task-conditioned grasp affordance region and generate feasible grasp-motion pairs with an arm-hand motion generator. Real-world experiments show improved 3D grounding accuracy and execution reliability over single-view RGB-D grounding and fine-tuned VLA baselines. We further demonstrate long-horizon manipulation through closed-loop status verification and replan, enabling zero-shot execution on unseen objects and tool-use tasks in novel scenes.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [ATIM: An ACT-R-Based Task Interface Model for Predicting Operator Action Time in Digital Nuclear Control Rooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18254)
*Xingyu Xiao Jonghyun Kim,Jiejuan Tong,Jingang Liang,Haitao Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出ATIM模型，一种基于ACT-R认知架构、融合视觉搜索理论、菲茨定律和认知架构原理的理论驱动框架，能够直接从界面特征（目标显著性、语义干扰）预测操作员在数字化控制室中的行动时间，无需额外用户实验。模型将行动时间分解为视觉、认知、运动和交互四个组件，并通过数据驱动聚类识别任务类型，针对新手和熟练用户分别估计参数，在核电站控制室数据集上达到3.17秒的平均绝对误差和0.664的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖大量实验数据或黑箱模型，可解释性和实用性受限，而数字化控制室中操作员绩效受界面特征强烈影响，亟需一种可解释、无需额外实验即可预测操作员行动时间的理论驱动建模方法。

Method: 基于ACT-R认知架构，将总行动时间分解为视觉、认知、运动和交互四个子组件；以目标显著性和语义干扰等界面特征作为直接输入；通过数据驱动聚类识别任务类型；分别估计新手和熟练用户的参数集；在数字核电站控制室数据集上校准和验证。

Result: 在保留验证集上，ATIM模型平均绝对误差为3.17秒，与观测性能强相关（r=0.664），对未见数据具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ATIM模型在保持可解释性的同时具备良好的泛化能力，为复杂人机系统的工效学评估和界面设计提供了新颖有效的工具。

Abstract: Human performance in digital control rooms is strongly influenced by interface characteristics, which shape visual search, cognitive processing, and motor execution. Accurate prediction of operator action time is therefore essential for ergonomic evaluation, interface design, and performance optimization in safety critical systems. However, existing approaches typically rely on extensive experimental data or black box models, limiting their interpretability and practical applicability. This study proposes ATIM (ACTR based Task Interface Model), a theory guided and data calibrated modeling framework that predicts operator action time directly from interface features. The model decomposes total action time into visual, cognitive, motor, and interaction components, integrating principles from visual search theory, Fitts law, and cognitive architecture modeling. Interface characteristics, including target salience and semantic interference, are used as direct inputs, enabling prediction without additional user experiments. A dataset from a digital nuclear control room environment was used for calibration and validation. Task types were identified through data driven clustering, and separate parameter sets were estimated for novice and experienced users. The proposed framework achieved a mean absolute error of 3.17 s and demonstrated strong correlation with observed performance (r = 0.664) on a held-out validation set. The results show that ATIM generalizes well to unseen data while maintaining interpretability, offering a novel tool for ergonomic assessment and interface design in complex human machine systems.

</details>


### [193] [Human-Machine Bidirectional Trust-Aware Analysis and Design for Human-Led Truck Platooning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18255)
*Chenzhao Li,Yunzhijun Yu,Yukun Lu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对人类驾驶卡车引领自动驾驶跟随车队的场景，提出了一个双向信任概念框架和定量模型，将人类对自动化的信任与自动化对人类信任的互惠关系形式化，并以跟车距离作为关键变量模拟信任的协同演化，最终为自主跟随车设计提供了信任校准指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注人类对自动化的单向信任，忽略了专业卡车司机与自动驾驶系统之间信任的互惠性，尤其缺乏对自动化系统如何"信任"并解读人类行为这一反向维度的探讨。

Method: 基于能力、善意、诚信等经典信任理论，结合卡车司机心理学，构建了双向信任概念框架；并引入以跟车距离为关键交互变量的定量模型，通过仿真示例展示信任的正向强化与负向螺旋效应。

Result: 仿真示例展示了信任双向反馈回路中的两种动态模式：积极增强（信任上升、跟车距离缩短）和消极螺旋（信任下降、跟车距离增大），验证了模型对信任协同演化机制的刻画能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为人因与工程视角的融合提供了理论基础和初步定量工具，并推导出自主跟随车辆的设计准则，以促进适当的信任校准、提升安全性和用户接受度。

Abstract: Human-led truck platooning, where a human-driven truck leads one or more autonomous followers, offers significant benefits in fuel efficiency, safety, and traffic flow. However, its successful deployment hinges on trust between the human driver and the automated systems. Unlike conventional automation, trust in this context is inherently bidirectional: the human must trust the autonomous followers, and the followers must reliably interpret and respond to the human's behavior. While prior research has extensively studied human trust in automation, the reciprocal nature of trust, especially considering the expertise of professional truck drivers, remains underexplored. This paper develops a conceptual framework of bidirectional trust for human-led platooning systems. Drawing on established trust theories (ability, benevolence, integrity) and insights from truck driver psychology, we propose distinct dimensions for human-to-automation trust and automation-to-human trust. To move beyond conceptualization, we introduce a quantitative model that operationalizes the bidirectional dynamics, using the following distance as the key interaction variable to illustrate how trust co-evolves through a feedback loop. Simulation examples demonstrate both positive reinforcement and negative spiral effects. Based on this framework and its quantitative instantiation, we derive design guidelines for autonomous followers to foster appropriate trust calibration, improve safety, and enhance user acceptance. The framework bridges human factors and engineering perspectives, providing a theoretical and preliminary quantitative foundation for future empirical and modeling research.

</details>


### [194] [Dynamic In-Group Persona Generation for Enhancing Human-AI Rapport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18256)
*Yoonseok Oh,Inseo Jung,Jinkyu Kim,Jungbeom Lee,Minwoo Kang,Suhong Moon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于内群体人格的LLM聊天机器人方法，通过识别用户主要关切并生成相似关切但不同背景的合成人格，显著提升了人机 rapport（融洽关系）和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM聊天机器人越来越多应用于咨询、同伴支持等人际领域，但建立人机融洽关系仍具挑战性，现有方法缺乏针对性的身份认同策略。

Method: 先识别用户主要关切和个人背景，再生成共享相似关切但年龄、职业等背景细节不同的合成内群体人格，通过人类被试实验与无人格条件及最小自我表露基线对比评估。

Result: 内群体人格代理在感知融洽度和个人相关性上显著优于两种基线条件，并带来更积极的用户体验，尤其是更高的参与度。

Conclusion: 基于内群体人格的条件化策略能有效增强人机融洽关系，为提升LLM在人际场景中的应用效果提供了可行路径。

Abstract: LLM-based chatbots are increasingly applied in interpersonal domains such as counseling and peer support, where establishing human-AI rapport is crucial yet remains challenging. In this work, we introduce a novel approach for conditioning LLMs with in-group personas, which (i) first identifies a user's primary concern and brief personal context (e.g., a computer science undergraduate worried about future career prospects), and (ii) generates a synthetic in-group persona that shares a similar primary concern while differing in background and narrative details, such as age or profession (e.g., a junior researcher at an AI startup). Furthermore, we conduct a human-subject study to systematically evaluate the effectiveness of in-group persona agents in enhancing human-AI rapport. We compare our approach against two baseline conditions: a conventional agent without persona conditioning and an agent exhibiting minimal self-disclosure (e.g., "I've felt that too"). Results from post-task questionnaires assessing rapport and user experience indicate that the in-group persona agent significantly improves perceived rapport and personal relevance compared to the baselines, and also yields more positive user experience-most notably higher engagement.

</details>


### [195] [From Memorization to Creation: Evaluating the Cognitive Depth of LLM-Generated Educational Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18257)
*Xiaolong Wang,Zhe Zhao,Song Lai,Chaoli Zhang,Zijie Geng,Yu Tong,Ye Wei,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过布鲁姆分类学视角评估了六种主流LLM生成高阶思维问题的能力，提出细粒度提示策略（降低重复率24.45%，提升高阶认知输出11.53%），引入认知跃迁强度（CogShift）和类别漂移等量化指标，并进行了可解释性分析，揭示了认知感知提示设计在个性化学习中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在教育内容自动化生成方面展现出潜力，但它们能否生成激发高阶思维（超越死记硬背、实现认知跃迁）的问题仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: （1）采用布鲁姆分类学框架，设计细粒度提示策略；（2）构建人机混合评估协议，在计算机科学、K-12数学和社会科学三个领域生成并分析20,700个问题；（3）提出认知跃迁强度（CogShift）和类别漂移等量化指标；（4）进行链式思维提示的可解释性分析。

Result: 细粒度提示策略使Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的问题重复率降低24.45%，InternLM3-8B-Instruct的高阶认知输出比例提升11.53%；InternLM3在多层级跃迁中表现最优；指标级相关性分析增强了链式思维提示的透明度。

Conclusion: 揭示了认知感知提示设计的重要性，为在个性化学习系统中部署LLM提供了基准参考。

Abstract: While LLMs show promise in automating educational content creation, their ability to generate questions that stimulate higher-order thinking remains understudied. This work evaluates six widely-used LLMs through a Bloom's Taxonomy lens, focusing on their capacity to transcend rote memorization and achieve cognitive leaps. Using a hybrid human--AI evaluation protocol, we generate and analyze 20{,}700 questions across computer science, K--12 math, and social-science domains. Key contributions include: (1) a fine-grained prompting strategy that reduces question repetitiveness by 24.45\% for Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and increases the proportion of higher-order cognitive level outputs by 11.53\% for InternLM3-8B-Instruct; (2) quantitative metrics for cognitive shift intensity (CogShift) and category drift, revealing InternLM3's superior performance in multi-level transitions; (3) an interpretability analysis revealing metric-level correlations that enhance the transparency of Chain-of-Thought prompting. Our findings highlight the importance of cognitive-aware prompt design and provide benchmarks for deploying LLMs in personalized learning systems.

</details>


### [196] [Examining Human-Like Behaviors in LLMs: A Multi-Dimensional Analysis of Model Behaviors, User Factors, and System Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18258)
*Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Margit Bowler,Leon A Gatys*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过21,000轮多轮对话分析，发现LLM类人行为普遍存在但受模型和用户因素影响；人类评估者认为LLM的自我参照和关系建立行为不如人类恰当，而边界维护行为比人类更恰当；系统提示可控制这些行为但需谨慎评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者和从业者缺乏方法和实证依据来决策LLM应该何时以及展示何种类型的类人行为。

Method: 采用LLM-as-a-judge和人类评估方法，对四个广泛使用的模型（gpt-4o, gpt-4.1-mini, claude-sonnet-4.6, gemini-2.5-flash）的21,000轮多轮对话进行多维度分析。

Result: 类人行为普遍存在但因模型和用户因素而异；自我参照和关系建立行为被人类评估者认为LLM展示不如人类恰当，而边界维护行为LLM展示比人类更恰当；系统提示可控制这些行为但需仔细评估以避免意外效果。

Conclusion: 为负责任的LLM设计与评估提供了建议，强调了需谨慎控制类人行为以避免不当效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit a wide range of human-like behaviors, from expressing thoughts and emotions, to engaging in relationship-building with users, to refusing requests and maintaining boundaries. Despite their prevalence, researchers and practitioners lack methods and empirical insights to make informed decisions about when and what types of human-like behaviors LLMs should exhibit. To fill this gap, we present a multi-dimensional analysis of the prevalence, potential effects, and controllability of these behaviors using LLM-as-a-judge and human evaluation. Across 21,000 multi-turn conversations from four widely used models (gpt-4o, gpt-4.1-mini, claude-sonnet-4.6, gemini-2.5-flash), we find that human-like behaviors are pervasive but vary across models and user factors (conversation goals and user profiles). In terms of perceived appropriateness, human evaluators judged self-referential and relationship-building behaviors as less appropriate from LLMs than from humans, but boundary-maintaining behaviors more appropriate from LLMs than from humans. Finally, we show that system prompting can control these behaviors, though it requires careful evaluation to avoid unintended effects. We discuss the implications of our findings and provide recommendations for responsible LLM design and evaluation.

</details>


### [197] [Caring Without Feeling: Affective Dynamics as the Control Layer of Human-AI Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18259)
*Junjie Xu,Xingjiao Wu,Zihao Zhang,Yujia Xu,Yuzhe Yang,Jin Zhu,Luwei Xiao,Wen Wu,Liang He*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文综述了情感动态在AI代理协作中的作用，提出将情感视为人机之间的协调层，而非AI的内部属性，用于协商能力、不确定性和责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算、大语言模型模拟共情、自动化信任及AI安全等领域的研究虽已揭示重要设计原则，但这些文献彼此孤立，缺乏统一的框架来解释情感线索如何在代理协作（即人类委托、监控和纠正关键任务的场景）中运作。

Method: 综合梳理了情感动态的计算机制和交互机制，分析了模型生成的情感信号如何进入影响依赖、修复和监督的交互循环，并提出了一个将情感视为协调层的框架。

Result: 提出了一个将情感视为协调层的框架，该框架为校准测量、目的性设计和知情治理提供了基础。

Conclusion: 情感不应被视为AI的内部属性，而应作为人类与代理之间协调能力、不确定性和责任的协调层，以此为基础进行测量、设计和治理。

Abstract: AI agents that plan, retain memory across sessions, invoke external tools and act with partial autonomy are transforming human--AI collaboration. Research on affective computing, simulated empathy in large language models, trust in automation and AI safety has illuminated important design principles, yet these literatures remain fragmented. No integrated account explains how affective cues operate within agentic collaboration -- settings in which humans delegate, monitor and correct consequential tasks. This Review synthesises computational and interactional mechanisms of affective dynamics: the processes through which affective cues, emotion-like behaviour and perceived agent affect shape trust calibration, delegation decisions, error correction, dependence and governance. We trace how model-generated affective signals enter interaction loops that govern reliance, repair and oversight, and propose a framework that treats affect not as an internal property of AI but as a coordination layer through which humans and agents negotiate capability, uncertainty and responsibility. The framework provides a foundation for calibrated measurement, purposeful design and informed governance.

</details>


### [198] [FluidViews: Adaptive Drag-and-Drop Token Filters for Heterogeneous Multi-View Visual Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18260)
*Bhanu Sunku*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: FluidViews是一个基于Web的交互式可视化分析框架，通过将过滤器提升为一等可操纵对象（Copy-as-Highlight和Drag-as-Filter两种直接操作交互），以及可选的弹出式微视图，实现了跨视图的流畅、多步探索，减少了认知负荷并保持了分析动量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的交互式可视化分析工作流中，刚性的过滤面板和上下文切换会打断分析师的认知流，导致分析动量中断。现有方法缺乏将过滤器作为可直接操纵对象来实现流畅、瞬态跨视图比较和原位过滤的能力。

Method: 提出了两种新颖的直接操作交互：1) Copy-as-Highlight——允许用户复制任何视觉标记为持久的高亮标记，用于快速瞬态跨视图比较；2) Drag-as-Filter——允许用户拾取标记并拖放到其他视图上，原位应用上下文敏感过滤器（无需菜单、面板或模态对话框）。同时设计了可选的弹出式微视图，提供按需的空间独立子视图进行详细检查。

Result: 通过将轻量级手势嵌入协调的多视图环境中，FluidViews保持了分析动量，减少了认知开销，支持跨异构数据集的流畅多步探索。论文描述了系统的设计与实现，并展示了其在探索性工作流中的应用。

Conclusion: 以有形过滤器对象为核心理念的交互设计能够变革交互式数据探索方式，通过将过滤器提升为可直接操纵的一等对象来打破传统过滤面板的局限，从而增强分析师的认知连续性和探索流畅性。

Abstract: Interactive visual analytics workflows are often disrupted by rigid filter panels and context switches that break analysts' cognitive flow. We introduce FluidViews, a web-based framework that elevates filters to first-class, manipulable objects through two novel direct-manipulation interactions. Copy-as-Highlight enables users to duplicate any visual mark into a persistent highlight token for rapid, transient cross-view comparison, while Drag-as-Filter allows analysts to pick up a mark and drop it onto another view to apply context-sensitive filters in place no menus, panels, or modal dialogs required. An optional pop-out micro-view provides on-demand, spatially independent subviews for detailed inspection without disrupting the primary workspace. By embedding these lightweight gestures into coordinated multi-view environments, FluidViews preserves analytic momentum, reduces cognitive overhead, and supports fluid, multi-step exploration across heterogeneous datasets. We describe the system's design and implementation, illustrate its application in exploratory workflows, and discuss how tangible filter objects can transform interactive data exploration.

</details>


### [199] ["Are you an AI?" Analyzing Client Suspicion of AI Use in Crisis Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18261)
*Shreya Shah,Akshay Swaminathan,Meghana Simhadri,Ivan Lopez,Sharang Phadke,Divyanjali Verma,Abhay John,Luke Zhao,Fiona Cai,Sharon Zhang,Gloria Ye,Ivy Pham,William Wang,Sebastian Garcia,Sarah Wornow,Angelina Wang,Nigam H. Shah*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过分析75,777条来自印度WhatsApp危机咨询热线（纯人工服务）的对话，发现怀疑AI参与的对话比例从2024年6月的0.8%上升至2025年3月的2.6%；21.5%的怀疑用户明确偏好人类咨询师；怀疑主要出现在对话前半段（68.3%）；当咨询师提供 reassurance 时，仍有17.6%的客户继续施压或终止对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具在心理健康领域部署日益增多，但公众信任度尚不明确，尤其缺乏对客户如何感知咨询中AI参与（特别是在需要共情与连接的危机时刻）的理解。

Method: 分析了75,777条来自印度WhatsApp热线（纯人工服务）的危机咨询对话，统计客户怀疑AI参与的比例、触发怀疑的因素及咨询师的回应方式。

Result: 怀疑AI的对话比例从0.8%（2024年6月）升至2.6%（2025年3月）；21.5%的怀疑客户明确偏好人类；68.3%的怀疑出现在对话前半段；咨询师提供 reassurance 后，17.6%的客户继续质疑或结束对话。

Conclusion: 随着AI工具逐步整合到咨询师工作流中，理解这些动态对于设计能维护治疗性关系的AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) tools get increasingly deployed for mental healthcare, public trust in these systems remains uncertain. It is unclear how clients perceive AI involvement in counseling interactions, particularly in moments of crisis that require empathy and connection. To address this gap, we analyzed 75,777 crisis counseling conversations from a human-staffed WhatsApp helpline in India to characterize how often clients suspected they were speaking to AI, what triggered those doubts, and how counselors responded. Though no conversations actually involved AI assistance, the proportion of conversations where clients suspected AI use increased from 0.8% in June 2024 to 2.6% in March 2025. Within suspicious conversations, 21.5% of clients stated an explicit preference for humans. Client suspicion primarily arose in the first half of messages (68.3%), and when counselors offered reassurance (e.g. 'I assure you; this is not ai!'), clients continued to press or ended the conversation 17.6% of the time. As AI tools get increasingly integrated into counselor workflows, understanding these dynamics is essential for designing AI systems that preserve the therapeutic relationship between counselors and clients.

</details>


### [200] [When Prompts Mislead: Textual Dominance and Diagnostic Bias in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18262)
*Inhyuk Park,Doohyun Park*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在眼科疾病判别任务中提示策略的失效模式。研究发现：模型保留粗粒度空间定位能力；单样本文本提示会引入偏差；当文本提示与视觉线索矛盾时，文本会覆盖正确视觉信息（准确率从75%降至46%），且链式思维推理会加剧而非修正错误。结论认为仅靠提示策略不足以支持MLLM安全临床应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管提示策略被广泛认为能支持诊断推理，但它们在医学MLLM中的潜在失效模式尚缺乏系统表征。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统揭示提示策略在医学影像判别任务中的局限性。

Method: 以开源眼科MLLM FundusExpert-1B为研究对象，在BRSET数据集的出血与玻璃膜疣判别任务上，设计三组受控实验：(i) 人工注入标记探测空间定位能力；(ii) 比较零样本与单样本文本提示的性能差异；(iii) 引入覆盖病变轮廓与矛盾文本声明的对抗性设置，评估多模态信息冲突时的模型行为。

Result: 模型保留粗粒度区域级空间定位；单样本提示使预测偏向提示的发现；当视觉轮廓与文本声明矛盾时，文本提示主导预测，准确率从75%降至46%，CoT推理进一步加剧性能下降而非实现自我修正。

Conclusion: 尽管局限于单一模型和数据集，但结果表明仅靠提示策略可能不足以保障医学MLLM的安全临床部署。提示策略存在系统性失效风险，需要更可靠的机制来确保多模态信息的正确处理。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly being evaluated for medical applications, where computational constraints often make prompting strategies the only practical alternative to fine-tuning. Such strategies are generally assumed to support diagnostic reasoning, yet their potential failure modes in medical MLLMs remain poorly characterized. We analyze FundusExpert-1B, an open-source ophthalmology MLLM, on a hemorrhage versus drusen discrimination task using the public BRSET dataset, adopted here as a controlled testbed for our analysis. (i) A controlled probe with artificially injected markers confirms that the model retains coarse, region-level spatial grounding. (ii) Compared with zero-shot inference, one-shot textual prompts bias predictions toward the prompted finding. (iii) When an overlaid lesion contour is paired with an inconsistent textual claim, the textual prompt overrides the correct visual cue: overall accuracy drops from 75% to 46% relative to the visual-only condition, and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning is associated with further degradation rather than self-correction. Although limited to a single model and dataset, our findings suggest that prompting strategies alone may be insufficient for the safe clinical deployment of medical MLLMs.

</details>


### [201] [How Well Do Large Language Models Capture Human Personality?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18263)
*Aanisha Bhattacharyya,Yaman Kumar Singla,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Changyou Chen,Jitendra Ajmera*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统验证了LLM人格模拟中的三个常见假设，发现增加人格描述丰富度反而导致“人格流形坍缩”——表征和行为多样性系统性下降。简单的人格描述（如年龄+性别）在模拟任务中优于复杂描述（如理想客户画像），但某些特定属性组合能保持行为稳定性，形成“对齐桥梁”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM人格提示模拟中三个未经验证的隐含假设：（1）更丰富的人格描述提升行为保真度；（2）同等规模的属性组合具有相同的可模拟性；（3）人格定义可跨任务泛化。

Method: 跨多种架构、模型规模和模拟设置，系统评估人格复杂度对表征和行为的影响，包括潜空间分离度分析、下游任务行为分化度量等。

Result: 发现“人格流形坍缩”现象：人格描述越复杂，潜空间中人格间分离度越小，行为多样性越弱。简单年龄-性别人格在跨行业预测准确率上显著优于复杂ICP。坍缩非均匀，某些属性组合形成“对齐桥梁”，保持行为稳定性。

Conclusion: 需采用表征感知的人格构建策略，而非简单增加人格描述丰富度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate human populations via persona prompting, often under the assumptions that richer persona descriptions improve behavioral fidelity, similarly sized attribute combinations are equally simulatable, and persona definitions generalize across tasks. In this work, we formalize these assumptions and systematically evaluate them across multiple architectures, scales, and simulation settings. We identify a fundamental limitation we term persona manifold collapse, where increasingly expressive persona specifications lead to systematic contraction of representational and behavioral diversity. Across models, increasing persona complexity consistently reduces inter-persona separation in latent space and weakens behavioral differentiation in downstream simulation tasks. These effects persist across multiple analyses as richer personas fail to preserve human subgroup disagreement, performance varies across attribute combinations of similar size, and adding descriptive detail often degrades rather than improves simulation fidelity. Surprisingly, simple Age-Gender personas consistently outperform richly specified Ideal Customer Profiles (ICPs) across industries, achieving substantially higher downstream prediction accuracy. We find that collapse is not uniform across attributes. Certain combinations remain behaviorally stable and preserve stronger alignment with human responses, forming localized regions we term alignment bridges. Together, our results provide empirical and conceptual foundations for understanding the limits of persona-conditioned simulation, highlighting the need for representation-aware persona construction rather than increasing persona expressivity alone.

</details>


### [202] [Synthetic Resonance: A Framework for Growth-Oriented Human-AI Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18265)
*Richard A. Fabes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"合成共振"（synthetic resonance）概念，作为理解人机关系的整合框架，描述无需共享感受或相互意识即可产生有意义关系的人机互动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言和理论无法准确描述人类与AI系统之间日益频繁和持久的关系，要么过度拟人化（赋予AI主观体验），要么将AI简化为工具或威胁，缺乏精确的概念工具。

Method: 概念分析与理论建构：提出"合成共振"作为整合框架，将人机关系定义为结构化的、动态的互动模式，不需要第二个体验主体即可产生关系感。

Result: 界定了"合成共振"的内涵：它描述的是人类定义的、有意义的关系如何在没有共享情感或相互意识的前提下，在人与AI系统之间产生。

Conclusion: 合成共振为人机关系提供了更精确的概念化方式，突显了其潜在价值与伦理意涵，并呼吁更多关于其过程和结果的实证研究。

Abstract: As human relationships with artificial intelligence systems become increasingly frequent and sustained, existing language and theory fail to accurately capture the nature of these affiliations. Common descriptors such as mutual understanding, connection, or friendship risk anthropomorphizing systems that lack subjective experience, while dominant frameworks tend to reduce AI to either a tool or a threat. In this paper, I introduce the concept of synthetic resonance as an integrative framework for understanding human-AI relationships. Synthetic resonance describes how relationships humans define as meaningful can emerge between a human and an AI system without the need to attribute shared feelings or mutual awareness. I argue that synthetic resonance is best understood as a structured, dynamic pattern of interaction that can produce a sense of relationship without the presence of a second experiencing subject. By clarifying this distinction, the concept of synthetic resonance offers a more precise way of conceptualizing human-AI relationships and highlights their potential value and ethical implications. I also call for more research that tests the processes and outcomes of synthetic resonance.

</details>


### [203] [EMORSION: Examining the Impact of Audio Parameters on Emotional Responses and Immersion in Film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18266)
*Nelly Garcia,Ruby Crocker,Bleiz M Del Sette,Fabrizio Smeraldi,Charalampos Saitis,George Fazekas,Joshua Reiss*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: EMORSION是一项探索性概念验证研究，通过操控频率、动态和空间定位三个音频设计参数，结合问卷、生理测量和视频运动追踪的多模态框架，验证了电影音频设计对观众情绪和沉浸感的影响，发现非常规混音导致观众解读差异更大，而传统沉浸式混音则带来更强的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究电影音频设计如何塑造观众的情绪和沉浸感，尤其是在影院环境中的具体影响机制。

Method: 选取4个电影场景（恐怖片2部、剧情片2部），分别制作多个替代性音频混音，系统操控频率、动态和方向性三个核心参数。三组观众分别观看每个场景的一种操控混音和对照混音，通过问卷自报情绪和沉浸感、心率监测及视频运动追踪进行多模态评估。

Result: 不同音频条件之间可测量且可解释的差异被成功捕获；非常规混音导致观众解读的变异性增大，而传统沉浸式混音则带来更强的观众间一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了EMORSION协议的有效性和可行性，为未来更大规模研究以刻画特定音频参数对观众体验的作用奠定了基础。

Abstract: EMORSION is an exploratory proof-of-concept study examining how film audio design shapes audience emotion and immersion in acinema setting. Four film scenes were selected across the horror (2) and drama (2) genres, balanced between mainstream and independent productions. For each scene, multiple alternative audio mixes were created by systematically manipulating three core aspects of audio design, frequency (pitch), dynamics (loudness), and directionality (spatial placement). Three audience groups viewed the scenes, with each group exposed to one manipulated mix alongside a control mix for each scene. Audience responses were assessed through a triangulated multimodal framework combining self-reported emotion and immersion via a questionnaire, physiological measures including heart rate monitoring, and video-based motion tracking. The protocol successfully captured measurable, interpretable differences across audio conditions, indicating that even subtle changes in audio design can shape emotional perception and immersion. Unconventional mixes tended to produce greater variability in audience interpretation, while conventional immersive mixes were associated with stronger cross-audience agreement. These findings establish the feasibility of the EMORSION protocol and motivate larger-scale studies to characterise the role of specific audio parameters in shaping audience experience.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond the Algorithm: Professional Experiences and Perceptions of AI Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18289)
*Micarah Malone-Gawu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对9名AI从业者的定性多案例研究，探讨了AI/ML系统中社会偏见的产生、感知与缓解方式，发现算法偏见源于历史不平等、排他性设计假设和组织压力，强调仅靠技术修正不足以确保公平，需要结构性问责、多元参与和持续的认知意识，建议将伦理嵌入早期设计、加强治理框架并培养反思性决策的制度环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的是审视社会偏见如何在AI/ML系统中产生、被感知以及如何被缓解，重点关注直接参与系统设计、开发和治理的从业者的实际经验与专业洞见。

Method: 采用定性多案例研究方法，基于交叉性理论和认知科学，运用解释主义视角，对9名从业者进行半结构化访谈，辅以文档分析和三角验证案例材料以丰富情境理解。

Result: 发现算法偏见源于历史不平等、排他性设计假设以及优先考虑速度与效率而非伦理反思的组织压力。参与者强调技术修正无法单独确保公平，公平的AI需要结构性问责、多元参与和开发全周期的持续认知意识。多数从业者指出伦理标准执行有限，组织文化对负责任实践的支持不一致。

Conclusion: 以人为本且具有社会基础的AI发展取决于将伦理嵌入早期设计、加强治理框架，以及培养鼓励反思性决策的制度环境。这些见解有助于负责任的AI的持续讨论，并为追求透明、问责且与受影响社区一致的AI系统设计提供实践指导。

Abstract: The purpose of this qualitative multi-case study was to examine how social bias emerges, is perceived, and can be mitigated within artificial intelligence and machine learning systems by practitioners directly involved in their design, development, and governance. Although examples from healthcare, criminal justice, employment, and education were used to illustrate domains where automated systems shape everyday life, the study focused on the lived experiences and professional insights of AI practitioners rather than sector-specific populations. Guided by Intersectionality Theory and Cognitive Science, the study employed an interpretivist approach, utilizing semi-structured interviews with nine practitioners, supplemented by document analysis and triangulated case material to enrich contextual understanding. Findings showed that algorithmic bias arises from historical inequities, exclusionary design assumptions, and organizational pressures that prioritize speed and efficiency over ethical reflection. Participants emphasized that technical corrections alone cannot ensure fairness; instead, equitable AI requires structural accountability, diverse participation, and sustained cognitive awareness during the development lifecycle. Many described limited enforcement of ethical standards and organizational cultures that inconsistently support responsible practice. The study concludes that human-centered and socially grounded AI development depends on embedding ethics early in the design process, strengthening governance frameworks, and cultivating institutional environments that encourage reflective decision-making. These insights contribute to ongoing conversations on responsible AI and offer practical guidance for organizations seeking to design systems that are transparent, accountable, and aligned with the communities they affect.

</details>


### [205] [Stitching the Divide: Investigating Mixed Reality as a Bridge Between Paper-Based and Digital Artifacts in UI/UX Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18511)
*Abidullah Khan,Jinghui Cheng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过访谈19位UI/UX设计师和9次概念探针用户研究，探讨了混合现实（MR）如何整合纸笔与数字设计流程，并提出了四个未来MR系统的设计维度，以支持更流畅、创意和协作的设计实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UI/UX设计师同时使用纸笔和数字工具，但缺乏无缝整合两者的工具，MR在这方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 首先对19位专业UI/UX设计师进行访谈，了解他们使用纸笔和数字工具的经验；随后组织9次概念探针（conceptual-probe）用户研究，让设计师体验结合纸笔和数字原型设计的MR探针，并脑暴MR在UI/UX设计中的潜力。

Result: 参与者认为MR能够支持连续混合设计工作流、减少手动重建、支持空间锚定工作空间、促进实时跨媒介协作；他们还设想了未来MR工具应具备AI辅助、更丰富的交互和动态内容、以及在统一环境中管理多样化设计工件的能力。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提炼出四个未来MR系统的设计维度，以实现更流畅、创意和协作的设计实践。

Abstract: UI/UX designers work with both paper-based and digital artifacts but lack tools that seamlessly integrate the two. Mixed Reality (MR) offers under-explored opportunities to combine the strengths of both design environments. To examine these opportunities, we first conducted interviews with 19 professional UI/UX designers to understand their current experiences using paper and digital artifacts. Motivated and informed by the interview insights, we organized nine conceptual-probe user study sessions in which designers engaged with a MR-probe that combined paper and digital prototyping processes and brainstormed MR's potential in UI/UX design. We found that participants valued MR for enabling continuous hybrid design workflows, reducing manual reconstruction, supporting spatially anchored workspaces, and facilitating real-time cross-medium collaboration. They also envisioned future MR tools with AI assistance, richer interactive and dynamic content, and the ability to manage diverse design artifacts within a unified environment. From these findings, we derive four design dimensions for future MR systems that could enable more fluid, creative, and collaborative design practices.

</details>


### [206] ["The New Era of Tech-Enabled Traceability": Tensions between the FDA's Data Governance Vision and the Lived Realities of Food Producers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18593)
*Soonho Kwon,Catherine Wieczorek,Heidi Biggs,Shellye Suttles,Tammi S. Etheridge,Annabel Rothschild,Shaowen Bardzell*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文分析了FDA食品追溯规则如何将农业食品供应链利益相关者重新配置为数据劳工，通过对1,198条公众评论的定性分析，揭示了数据驱动政策实施对小型和资源不足利益相关者造成的三重张力：劳动、财务和教育负担；基础设施限制下的不可行性；以及规则模糊性带来的困惑与负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDA通过食品追溯规则设想建立技术赋能的追溯体系以快速识别和移除受污染食品，从而降低食源性疾病风险。然而，该规则对数据采集、格式化和报告提出了严格规定，将利益相关者重新配置为数据劳工，可能对小型、资源不足的参与者造成不成比例的负担。

Method: 以数据女性主义（Data Feminism）为理论取向，对Regulations.gov上针对拟议规则提交的1,198条公众评论进行定性文档分析，关注数据驱动政策实施如何对不同利益相关者产生差异化影响。

Result: 识别出三个关键张力：1) 利益相关者在被重新配置为数据工作者时面临的个体劳动、财务和教育负担；2) 因基础设施限制、文化背景和情境化生产实践导致数据追踪不可行的情况；3) 规则本意提供的灵活性因其模糊性反而引入困惑和负担。

Conclusion: FDA食品追溯规则虽然旨在通过技术赋能提升食品安全，但其强制执行的数据收集要求实际上将利益相关者异化为数据劳工，尤其对小型和资源不足的参与者产生不公平负担，揭示了数据驱动监管政策在实施中的结构性不平等问题。

Abstract: The U.S. Food and Drug Administration (FDA)'s Food Traceability Rule requires agri-food supply chain stakeholders (stakeholders)--including farmers, fishers, retail workers, and others--to maintain detailed tracking records beginning in January 2026. Through this Rule, the FDA envisions a "New Era of Tech-Enabled Traceability," in which standardized, harmonized tracking data serve as a foundational public health infrastructure, enabling more rapid identification and removal of potentially contaminated food and ultimately reducing the risk of foodborne illness.
  Despite this promising vision, we observe that the Rule reconfigures agri-food stakeholders into data laborers by mandating stringent data collection, formatting, and reporting requirements. In this paper, we examine the tensions and burdens that arise from such reconfiguration. Leveraging Data Feminism as an orientation to attend to how data-driven policy implementation disproportionately burdens smaller, under-resourced stakeholders who lack the infrastructural and financial capacity to comply, we analyze 1,198 public comments submitted to Regulations.gov in response to the proposed Rule. Our qualitative document analysis reveals three key tensions: (1) the individual labor, financial, and educational burdens stakeholders experience as they are reconfigured into data workers; (2) moments where data tracking becomes infeasible due to infrastructural limitations, cultural contexts, and situated production practices; and (3) instances where the Rule's intended flexibility instead introduces confusion and burden due to its ambiguity.

</details>


### [207] [Better Adherence, Richer Context: A Field Evaluation of LLM-Powered Conversational Voice Diaries for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18596)
*Amama Mahmood,Bokyung Kim,Honghao Zhao,Molly E. Atwood,Luis F. Buenaver,Michael T. Smith,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文设计并评估了一个基于LLM的对话式语音睡眠日记系统，通过智能音箱主动提示、结构化对话和自适应追问收集睡眠数据。在30名大学生的四周田野实验中，与文本日记相比，语音日记展现了更高的依从性和更丰富的上下文信息，但部分结构化字段的完整性较低，揭示了表达丰富度与结构化精度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统睡眠日记存在填写依从性低、静态表格难以捕捉夜间睡眠变化的上下文信息等问题，需要一种更自然、更具交互性的数据采集方式。

Method: 设计基于LLM的对话式语音日记系统，通过智能音箱进行早晚主动提示和自适应对话；在30名大学生中开展为期四周的组间田野实验，与使用匹配条目的文本移动日记进行对比。

Result: 语音日记的依从性更高，能收集到更详细的上下文信息（如日常习惯、压力源、环境因素）；用户认为其更易融入日常生活；但部分结构化字段的完整性低于文本日记。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的对话式语音助手在纵向健康自我报告中兼具优势与挑战，即在提升依从性和信息丰富度的同时，需解决结构化数据完整性问题。

Abstract: Sleep diaries are central to behavioral sleep medicine and cognitive behavioral therapy for insomnia, yet daily completion is difficult to sustain, and static forms often provide limited context for interpreting night-to-night sleep variation. We designed an LLM-powered conversational voice diary that delivers clinically grounded morning and evening sleep diary questions through proactive smart-speaker prompts, structured conversational intake, and adaptive follow-up dialogue. We evaluated the system in a four-week between-subjects field study with 30 university students, comparing it with a text-based mobile diary using matched diary items, reporting windows, and reminder intervals. Compared with the text-based diary, the conversational voice diary showed higher adherence and elicited more detailed contextual self-report about routines, stressors, environmental conditions, and other sleep-related factors. Participants also described the voice diary as easier to integrate into daily routines, despite longer perceived completion time. However, voice-based conversational intake produced lower completeness for some structured diary fields, revealing a trade-off between expressive richness and structured precision. These findings show both the promise and the challenge of using LLM-powered conversational voice assistants for longitudinal health self-report.

</details>


### [208] [HANSEL: Extracting Breadcrumbs from Web Agent Trajectories for Interactive Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18671)
*Yujin Zhang,Daye Nam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HANSEL是一种从AI web智能体轨迹中提取交互式、可验证证据的系统，通过保留页面状态的导航视图帮助用户验证智能体行为，在技术评估中达到83.7%精确率和88.8%召回率，减少61.6%轨迹数据量，并在用户研究中显著降低了任务完成时间和感知努力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI web智能体的透明度机制（完整轨迹日志、源代码链接、截图、LLM生成的摘要）将验证视为被动阅读任务，用户要么需要筛选海量日志，要么只能信任可能不准确的解释。因此需要一种将验证从被动消费转变为主动交互的方法。

Method: HANSEL从智能体轨迹中提取证据页面和片段，以可导航、交互式视图呈现，保留相关页面状态（如应用的过滤器、搜索查询和滚动位置）。当智能体的答案无法追溯到任何访问页面时，系统明确标记此缺口。

Result: 在AssistantBench和Online-Mind2Web的45个任务上，HANSEL达到83.7%精确率和88.8%召回率，轨迹数据量减少61.6%。14名参与者的用户研究表明，与标准智能体界面相比，HANSEL显著降低了任务完成时间和感知努力，在可用性、验证易用性和错误识别方面评分显著更高。

Conclusion: 将验证重新定义为交互活动而非被动消费智能体解释，能够实现更高效的人类对AI智能体的监督。

Abstract: AI web agents can perform complex, multi-step tasks such as searching for products, comparing options, and making purchases on behalf of users. However, verifying the correctness of an agent's output remains difficult. Existing transparency mechanisms, including full trajectory logs, source links, screenshots, and LLM-generated summaries, treat verification as a passive reading task, leaving users to sift through overwhelming logs or trust potentially unfaithful explanations. We present HANSEL (Highlighting Agent Navigation Steps as Evidence Links), a system that extracts interactive, verifiable evidence from web-agent trajectories. Given an agent trajectory, HANSEL extracts evidence pages and snippets and presents them as navigable, interactive views with relevant page state preserved (e.g., applied filters, search queries, and scroll positions), enabling users to verify how the agent arrived at its answer. When the agent's answer cannot be traced to any visited page, HANSEL explicitly flags this gap. A technical evaluation on 45 tasks from AssistantBench and Online-Mind2Web shows that HANSEL achieves 83.7% precision and 88.8% recall in identifying evidence pages, while reducing trajectory volume by 61.6%. In a controlled user study with 14 participants, HANSEL significantly reduced task completion time and perceived effort compared to a standard agent interface, while participants rated it significantly higher on usability, verification ease, and error identification. Our results demonstrate that reframing verification as an interactive activity, rather than passive consumption of agent explanations, leads to more efficient human oversight of AI agents.

</details>


### [209] [Human-AI Agent Interaction in a Business Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18716)
*Kathrin Paimann,Elizangela Valarini,Sebastian Juhl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究采用混合方法探索人-AI代理交互模式，识别积极用户体验的原则与测量方法，并通过大规模调查实验验证设计元素的有效性，旨在优化商业环境中的人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理深度融入核心业务流程，理解并设计有效的人机交互模式对价值创造至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性的用户体验原则和测量方法。

Method: 采用混合研究方法，结合定性与定量技术探索交互模式，并基于探索性研究发现设计大规模调查实验来评估具体设计元素的有效性。

Result: 识别出用户期望与需求，为促进AI代理采纳、建立信任和支持以用户为中心的决策提供了基础性发现；调查实验评估了特定设计元素在大规模场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究为商业环境中更直观、更有效的人-AI代理交互开发奠定了理论基础，有助于指导开发团队进行用户中心化的决策设计。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly integrated into core business processes, understanding and designing effective interaction patterns between humans and AI agents becomes crucial for value creation. This study identifies and evaluates principles and criteria for a positive User Experience (UX) with AI agents, along with methods for its measurement. We identify user expectations and needs to facilitate adoption, build trust, and support user-centered decision-making by development teams. Using a mixed-methods approach that combines qualitative and quantitative techniques, we explore interaction patterns between humans and AI agents. The findings from this exploratory research serve as the basis to develop a survey experiment which evaluates the effectiveness of specific design elements on a larger scale. This foundational research contributes to the development of more intuitive and effective human-AI agent interactions in business settings.

</details>


### [210] [SwitchBraidNet: Quantisation-Aware Lightweight Architecture for Hybrid Brain-Computer Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18816)
*Gourav Siddhad,Yogesh Kumar Meena*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出SwitchBraidNet，一种专为低功耗嵌入式设备设计的紧凑型混合BCI分类架构，通过双路径时间编织、自适应空间门控和对数方差读出层，在INT8精度下仅需3.03 KB存储，在OpenBMI数据集上实现MI 69.49%（FP16）、SSVEP 93.48%（FP32）的准确率及64.82 bits/min的混合信息传输率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合BCI（MI+SSVEP）虽能提供高维神经解码，但通常超出嵌入式硬件的计算限制，需要在保持高精度的同时实现极低资源消耗的轻量级模型。

Method: 提出SwitchBraidNet：①双路径时间编织模块提取多尺度振荡特征；②自适应挤压激励空间开关实现电极门控；③对数方差读出层直接编码频带功率。采用系统化量化感知训练，在FP32/FP16/INT8精度下与四种基线模型对比。

Result: MI准确率69.49%（FP16）、SSVEP准确率93.48%（FP32）、混合信息传输率64.82 bits/min（FP16）；INT8量化后仅3.03 KB，且在不同数值精度下均保持高准确率。

Conclusion: SwitchBraidNet在极低资源占用（3.03 KB INT8）下实现了混合MI-SSVEP BCI的高效分类，验证了其适用于低功耗嵌入式BCI部署的可行性。

Abstract: Hybrid brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) that integrate motor imagery (MI) and steady-state visual evoked potentials (SSVEP) provide high-dimensional neural decoding but typically exceed the computational limits of embedded hardware. To address this, we propose SwitchBraidNet, a compact EEG classification architecture designed for low-power deployment. The model employs a dual-path temporal braid to extract multiscale oscillatory features, an adaptive squeeze-and-excitation spatial switch for electrode gating, and a log-variance readout layer for direct band-power encoding. Furthermore, through systematic quantisation-aware training on the OpenBMI dataset, we compared SwitchBraidNet against four established baselines across FP32, FP16, and INT8 precisions. Experimental results demonstrate superior efficiency and performance, achieving MI accuracy of 69.49% (FP16), SSVEP accuracy of 93.48% (FP32), and a hybrid information transfer rate of 64.82 bits/min (FP16). With an INT8 footprint of only 3.03 KB, SwitchBraidNet maintains high accuracy across varying numerical precisions, demonstrating its suitability for low-power embedded BCI deployment.

</details>


### [211] [Improving Human-Robot Teamwork in Urban Search and Rescue Through Episodic Memory of Prior Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18836)
*Taewoon Kim,Emma van Zoelen,Mark Neerincx*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何让机器人利用先前团队协作中提取的协作模式（CPs）作为知识图谱 episodic memory，通过图表示学习选择最有效的记忆进行初始化，从而在人类-机器人团队合作中提升表现。实验表明，在MATRX USAR环境中，该方法将救援成功率从25.7%提升至41.3%，平均任务时间减少283秒，尤其在交互初期效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的人机团队协作需要机器人从交互一开始就能适应伙伴、情境和任务动态。然而，机器人缺乏利用先前团队协作经验的能力，因此研究如何将历史协作模式作为可复用的经验记忆，帮助机器人在新合作中成为更好的队友。

Method: 在MATRX USAR环境中，通过聊天和反思界面让人类外化协作模式（CPs）。将历史CPs表示为知识图谱 episodic memory，采用图表示学习结合节点分类目标，识别出最具代表性和有效性的记忆以供复用。在新协作回合开始前，用该记忆初始化机器人。

Result: 20名参与者、160轮观察中，用自动选择的单一历史CP初始化机器人，救援成功率从25.7%提升至41.3%，平均任务时间减少283秒。最强改进出现在交互初期。

Conclusion: 可复用的episodic memory能够帮助机器人在进入协作时具备更有效的任务知识，支持更顺畅的早期团队协作。

Abstract: Effective human-robot teamwork requires robots to adapt to partners, situations, and task dynamics from the start of an interaction. In the MATRX Urban Search and Rescue (USAR) environment, people can externalize collaboration patterns (CPs) they discover during teamwork through a chat and reflection interface. We study whether a robot can use such prior team experience to become a better teammate in future interactions. To this end, we represent historical CPs as knowledge-graph episodic memories and use graph representation learning with a node-classification objective to identify a representative and effective memory for reuse. We then initialize the robot with this memory before a new collaboration episode begins. Across 20 participants and 160 round-level observations, initializing the robot with a single automatically selected prior CP increases rescue success from 25.7% to 41.3% and reduces average task time by 283 seconds. The strongest gains appear at the beginning of interaction, suggesting that reusable episodic memory can help robots enter collaboration with more effective task knowledge and support smoother early teamwork.

</details>


### [212] [A Clinician-Centered Pipeline for Annotation and Evaluation in Ultrasound AI Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19174)
*Fangyijie Wang,Jianjun Yu,Wentao Shi,Haixia Huang,Ran Shi,Guénolé Silvestre,Kathleen M. Curran*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向超声影像AI研究的临床中心评估流程，通过集中式服务器和轻量级浏览器界面支持远程标注、盲法排序和多评分者参与，并在胎儿超声分割研究中验证了其有效性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像平台主要聚焦于数据集标注，缺乏对盲法模型比较和可重复评估工作流的集成支持，而临床医师中心的评估对验证医学AI系统至关重要，尤其在超声影像中定量指标无法全面捕捉临床可用性。

Method: 提出基于集中式服务器和轻量级浏览器界面的临床中心评估流程，支持远程标注、盲法排名、多评分者参与、集中结果聚合和自动统计分析，无需本地下载数据集。

Result: 在胎儿超声分割研究中，六名评分者（专家、通才、非专家）参与，系统自动生成了Spearman相关、Kendall's τ和Top-1选择统计量，结果显示专家与其他组之间存在中等到强一致性，盲法评估表明后期主动学习模型更受偏好。

Conclusion: 该流程能够支持超声影像中临床中心标注和可重复的人机AI评估研究，代码已开源在GitHub上。

Abstract: Clinician-centered evaluation is critical for validating medical AI systems, especially in ultrasound imaging where quantitative metrics do not always capture clinical usability. Existing medical image platforms primarily focus on dataset labeling. They lack integrated support for blinded model comparison and reproducible evaluation workflows. We present a clinician-centered pipeline for remote annotation and evaluation in ultrasound AI studies. The proposed pipeline uses a centralized server and lightweight browser interfaces to enable clinicians to perform annotation, blinded ranking, and review without local dataset downloads. The pipeline also supports multi-rater participation, centralized result aggregation, and automated statistical analysis. We validate the pipeline in a fetal ultrasound segmentation study with six raters spanning expert, generalist, and non-expert experience levels. The system automatically generated Spearman correlation, Kendall's $τ$, and top-1 selection statistics. Results indicated moderate to strong agreement across experts and other groups. The blinded evaluation results showed a tendency for later active learning models to be preferred. These outcomes suggest that the pipeline can support clinician-centered annotation and reproducible human-\ac{AI} evaluation studies in ultrasound imaging. The proposed pipeline is available on \href{https://github.com/13204942/SonoRate}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [213] [A Taxonomy of Mental Health and Technology Needs for Alzheimer's and Dementia Caregivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19247)
*Keran Wang,Drishti Goel,Jiayue Melissa Shi,Violeta J. Rodriguez,Daniel S. Brown,Dong Whi Yoo,Ravi Karkar,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆症（AD/ADRD）的非正式照护者（即"隐形第二患者"）面临的心理健康问题，提出了一套"照护者心理健康与技术分类法"，系统地将照护者需求与数字/AI技术干预措施相匹配，识别现有技术支持的缺口，并为人本化的痴呆照护创新提供跨学科共享框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将照护者复杂的心理社会经历简化为单一的"照护负担"构念，掩盖了哪些具体需求未得到满足或已得到有效支持；同时，数字与AI技术虽快速发展，但医学、心理学和技术研究之间缺乏共享框架，制约了累积性进展。

Method: 通过跨学科文献综述和两项针对照护者的定性研究，构建了照护者心理健康与技术分类法，系统关联AD/ADRD照护者需求与相应的技术干预类别。

Result: 识别了照护者优先需求与现有技术支持之间的不匹配；发现关系紧张和同情疲劳等领域尚未得到充分服务；提出了自适应响应系统的设计方向。

Conclusion: 该分类法为临床医生、研究人员和技术设计者提供了共享词汇，以指导在痴呆照护领域开发更以人为中心、更具临床基础的创新方案。

Abstract: Family members caring for individuals with Alzheimer's disease and related dementias (AD/ADRD) provide the foundation of long-term care worldwide. In 2023, more than 11 million U.S. family and friends contributed 18 billion hours of unpaid care, often at the cost of their own physical and mental health. These informal caregivers -- also referred as the "invisible second patients" -- experience elevated rates of mental health problems. Yet research commonly reduces their complex psychosocial experiences to a single construct of caregiver burden, obscuring which specific needs are unmet or effectively supported. At the same time, digital and AI-enabled technologies are rapidly expanding, from smartphone apps and videoconferencing to sensor platforms and AI chatbots. However, the absence of shared frameworks across medicine, psychology, and technology research limits cumulative progress. This study introduces a Caregiver Mental Health and Technology Taxonomy that systematically links AD/ADRD caregiver needs with corresponding classes of technology-based interventions. Drawing from an interdisciplinary literature review and two qualitative studies with caregivers, the taxonomy identifies mismatches between caregiver priorities and existing technological support, highlights under-served domains such as relational strain and compassion fatigue, and proposes design directions for adaptive, responsive systems. The framework offers a shared vocabulary to guide clinicians, researchers, and technology designers in developing more person-centered and clinically grounded innovation in dementia care.

</details>


### [214] [Correct Yourself, Keep My Trust: How Self-Correction and Social Connection Shape Credibility in Social Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19286)
*Biswadeep Sen,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 社交聊天机器人犯错误后，自我纠错能在不损害其可信度的前提下有效纠正错误，而外部纠错虽能纠正信息但会损害机器人的可信度；用户与机器人的社交联系强度能显著预测信念改变幅度，但仅在自我纠错时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交聊天机器人日益普及但易产生不准确信息，它们与用户建立的社交联系使得错误后果尤为严重。现有研究缺乏对社交聊天机器人错误恢复策略的系统比较，尤其是自我纠错与外部纠错在维护可信度方面的差异。

Method: 采用组间实验设计（N=120），比较三种纠错策略：网页撤回、同一社交聊天机器人自我纠错、专家聊天机器人纠错。测量可信度、感知专业性、社交吸引力和自我披露等变量。

Result: 三种策略均能同样有效地纠正错误，但只有自我纠错不会损害聊天机器人的可信度；自我纠错的聊天机器人在可信度和感知专业性上显著高于外部纠错。社交联系强度在自我纠错条件下显著预测信念改变，外部纠错则完全切断此关联。

Conclusion: 社交聊天机器人应自我纠错而非外包纠错；投资于社交联系是放大纠错有效性的功能性机制，而非单纯的设计特征。

Abstract: When social chatbots make mistakes, and they do, how they recover determines whether users trust them again. Social chatbots are increasingly integrated into everyday life, yet they remain prone to generating convincing but inaccurate information. The social connection they build with users makes such errors particularly consequential. We conducted a between-subjects experiment (N=120) comparing three error correction strategies: a webpage retraction, self-correction by the same social chatbot, and correction by an expert chatbot. Our results reveal two key findings. First, all three strategies corrected the error equally well, but only self-correction did so without damaging the chatbot's credibility: participants rated self-correcting chatbots significantly higher in both trustworthiness and perceived expertise than chatbots whose errors were corrected by external sources. Second, the strength of the user's social connection with the chatbot, measured through social attraction and self-disclosure, significantly predicted the magnitude of belief change, but only when the chatbot corrected itself. Outsourcing corrections to an external source severed this link entirely. These findings suggest that social chatbots should correct their own mistakes rather than outsource corrections, and that investing in social connection is a functional mechanism that amplifies correction effectiveness, not merely a design feature. We discuss implications for designing chatbots that maintain long-term credibility while effectively addressing their own errors.

</details>


### [215] [Integrating Multi-Label Classification and Generative AI for Scalable Analysis of User Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23018)
*Sandra Loop,Erik Bertram,Sebastian Juhl,Martin Schrepp*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一家大型软件公司在长期UX测量项目中开发的技术，包括：使用监督机器学习对用户评论进行主题标注、利用生成式AI创建简洁摘要、以及分析评论情感与产品满意度的关系。研究发现，情感分析不能可靠反映用户满意度，产品满意度需要通过问卷显式评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞争激烈的软件市场中，用户体验评估对保证软件质量和促进长期产品成功至关重要。然而，分析大量用户评论既困难又耗时，因此需要自动化技术来高效处理和解读大规模的用户反馈文本。

Method: 1）采用监督机器学习方法为每条评论分配预定义的主题标签；2）利用生成式AI（GenAI）创建用户反馈的简洁摘要；3）分析用户评论中的情感是否可作为整体产品满意度的指标。

Result: 情感分析本身不能可靠地反映用户满意度；产品满意度需要通过在调查中显式评估来测量用户对产品的感知。

Conclusion: 自动化主题标注和GenAI摘要生成可以有效处理大规模用户评论，但情感分析不足以替代显式的满意度测量，产品满意度仍需通过问卷直接调查获取。

Abstract: In highly competitive software markets, user experience (UX) evaluation is crucial for ensuring software quality and fostering long-term product success. Such UX evaluations typically combine quantitative metrics from standardized questionnaires with qualitative feedback collected through open-ended questions. While open-ended feedback offers valuable insights for improvement and helps explain quantitative results, analyzing large volumes of user comments is challenging and time-consuming. In this paper, we present techniques developed during a long-term UX measurement project at a major software company to efficiently process and interpret extensive volumes of user comments. To provide a high-level overview of the collected comments, we employ a supervised machine learning approach that assigns meaningful, pre-defined topic labels to each comment. Additionally, we demonstrate how generative AI (GenAI) can be leveraged to create concise and informative summaries of user feedback, facilitating effective communication of findings to the organization and especially upper management. Finally, we investigate whether the sentiment expressed in user comments can serve as an indicator for overall product satisfaction. Our results show that sentiment analysis alone does not reliably reflect user satisfaction. Instead, product satisfaction needs to be assessed explicitly in surveys to measure the user's perception of the product.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [Gaussian Mixture Attention: Linear-Time Sequence Mixing via Probabilistic Latent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18283)
*Yongchao Huang,Hassan Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯混合注意力（GMA），一种用K个高斯混合组件替代逐对点积的线性复杂度注意力机制，通过责任向量路由实现O(NK)存储，在长上下文任务中表现竞争性，但尚未超越优化的softmax注意力和状态空间模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力的密集token间交互模式导致O(N²)复杂度，成为Transformer架构扩展到长上下文的瓶颈。

Method: GMA将查询和键映射到K个高斯混合组件的后验责任向量上，通过责任空间中的重叠定义隐式亲和度，值通过K槽潜在内存读写。利用矩阵乘法结合律避免显式N×N亲和矩阵，存储复杂度为O(NK)。设计了双向和因果变体，并提供了端到端可微的参数化方法。

Result: GMA实现了固定K的线性内存缩放；在长上下文分类任务上与注意力基线竞争；因果GMA在WikiText-103上优于线性/随机特征注意力变体，但落后于优化的因果SDPA和Mamba。学习到的责任向量展示出广泛的组件使用和中等的词形类别对齐。

Conclusion: GMA作为一种概率性、可解释、固定K线性时间的注意力替代方案，而非优化softmax注意力或状态空间模型的通用替代品。

Abstract: The dense token-to-token interaction pattern of standard dot-product attention remains a central bottleneck in scaling Transformer architectures to long contexts. We introduce \textbf{Gaussian Mixture Attention (GMA)}, a probabilistic attention-style sequence mixer that replaces explicit pairwise query--key comparison with routing through $K$ learned Gaussian mixture components. Queries and keys are mapped to posterior \textit{responsibility} vectors over a shared latent routing space; their overlap defines an implicit responsibility-space affinity, while values are written into and read from a $K$-slot latent memory. By exploiting the associativity of matrix multiplication, GMA avoids materializing the induced $N\times N$ affinity matrix and instead uses two responsibility matrices whose dominant activation storage scales as $\mathcal{O}(NK)$ rather than $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ for fixed $K$. We formulate bidirectional and causal variants of GMA, provide an end-to-end differentiable parameterization of the Gaussian mixture components, and analyze its responsibility-modulated gradient structure, constrained non-negative low-rank affinity interpretation, and local routing stability. Empirically, GMA exhibits the intended fixed-$K$ linear memory scaling and is competitive with attention-style baselines on long-context classification, while causal GMA improves over tested linear/random-feature attention variants on WikiText-103 but remains behind optimized causal SDPA and Mamba in the current implementation. Analysis of learned responsibilities further shows broad component usage and moderate alignment with surface-form token categories, supporting GMA as a probabilistic, interpretable, fixed-$K$ linear-time attention-style alternative rather than a universal replacement for optimized softmax attention or state-space models.

</details>


### [217] [Breaking the Solver Bottleneck: Training Task Generators at the Learnable Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18284)
*Lorenz Wolf,Connor Watts,Roger Creus Castanyer,Geoffrey Bradway,Maxwill Lin,Augustine N. Mavor-Parker,Matthew Daborn-Sargent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PROPEL框架，通过训练轻量级激活探针作为求解器通过率的代理，在不需求解器在线回滚的情况下，高效训练任务生成器生成处于可学习前沿的任务，显著提升数学、代码和软件工程任务中处于目标求解率区间的任务比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练智能体的瓶颈正从模型能力转向前沿任务供给——固定任务分布会随着模型提升而饱和，而朴素合成生成的任务要么过于简单、要么无法求解或不适定。直接用RL优化任务生成器需对每个候选任务进行求解器回滚，对于软件工程任务单次回滚可能耗时数十分钟，求解器在环的生成器训练不可行。

Method: PROPEL采用求解器摊销框架：首先在一次性标注的生成任务与求解器结果语料上训练轻量级激活探针，该探针从冻结的生成器参考模型中预测目标求解器的通过率；在生成器优化时，探针作为求解率的代理，将生成器评估简化为单次前向传播，避免昂贵的求解器回滚。

Result: 在编码任务中，可学习前沿任务比例从10.1%提升至20.0%（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct求解器），从5.3%提升至12.6%（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct求解器）。在软件工程任务中，目标求解率区间生成比例从9.8%提升至19.6%（Qwen3.5-27B，在探针和生成器训练未见的代码仓库上）。

Conclusion: PROPEL框架能有效将任务生成导向目标求解率，在多种模型规模和任务领域（数学、代码、软件工程）上均取得显著提升，且泛化到未见过的软件工程仓库，证明了求解器摊销策略在可学习前沿任务生成中的有效性。

Abstract: The limiting resource for training agents via reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly frontier task supply: valid, solvable tasks just difficult enough to train the current model. As reasoning and agentic models improve, fixed task distributions saturate, while naive synthetic generation yields tasks that are trivial, impossible, or ill-posed. Training a task generator with RL to optimize validity and learnability can address this bottleneck, but direct optimization requires repeated solver rollouts per candidate. For software-engineering (SWE) tasks, a single rollout can take tens of minutes; solver-in-the-loop generator training is intractable. We introduce PROPEL, a solver-amortized framework for training task generators at the targeted solve rate. PROPEL trains a lightweight activation probe on a one-time labeled corpus of generated tasks and solver outcomes. The probe predicts target-solver pass rate from a frozen generator reference model and serves as a proxy for solve rate during generator optimization, reducing generator evaluation to a single forward pass. Across math, code, and software-engineering at multiple model scales, PROPEL shifts generation toward the targeted solve rate: for coding, tasks generated at the learnable frontier increase from $10.1\% \rightarrow 20.0\%$ for a Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct solver and from $5.3\% \rightarrow 12.6\%$ for a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct solver. For SWE, PROPEL increases the share of generations at the targeted solve rate from $9.8\% \rightarrow 19.6\%$ for Qwen3.5-27B on repositories not seen during training of probe and generator.

</details>


### [218] [CODEBLOCK: Learning to Supervise Code at the Right Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18286)
*Zhijie Deng,Ling Li,Jinlong Pang,Kaiqin Hu,Qi Xuan,Zhaowei Zhu,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CodeBlock提出了一种结构感知的稀疏监督框架，通过选择结构完整的代码块而非孤立token进行监督微调，在仅使用1.9%监督token的情况下，在六个代码生成基准上取得了优于全token SFT的pass@1结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言SFT中的token级选择方法不能直接迁移到代码领域，因为代码依赖于结构完整性和定义-使用关系，打断代码的语法语义完整性会破坏程序单元。

Method: 首先筛选高质量指令-响应对，然后将代码响应划分为语法连贯的编码项，通过聚合核心逻辑token上的广义交叉熵评估其效用，并利用数据流可达信号和桥接信号对编码项进行重排序，优先选择传播或连接重要程序依赖的块。训练时完整响应作为上下文，但损失仅应用于选中的代码项和信息性自然语言token。

Result: 在六个代码生成基准上，CodeBlock使用仅1.9%的监督响应token取得了比全token SFT和竞争性选择基线更强的平均pass@1。

Conclusion: 结构感知的稀疏监督方法可以有效应用于代码LLM的微调，在极少量监督token下达到更优性能，验证了代码结构完整性对监督信号选择的重要性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of code LLMs typically applies uniform cross-entropy loss to all response tokens, implicitly assuming that every token provides equally useful learning signal. Recent token-level selection methods challenge this assumption in natural-language SFT by supervising only high-value tokens. However, directly transferring token-level masking to code can break syntactically and semantically coherent program units, because code depends on structural completeness and definition-use relations.
